# Israeli audio: Hebrew, English, and phonetics

Read the Hebrew **right to left: ←**. Its English meaning is below it; the phonetic reading is below the English. Click a filename to open that audio file.

The stable filenames and stable audio IDs remain unchanged. `m` is Ari; `f` is Noa. The same word is listed once per voice recording.

**Words:** 2381 clips. **Reading lines:** 401 clips. English and phonetic labels are also available in [INDEX.csv](INDEX.csv) for every produced clip in the branch.

Phonetics use the project’s siddur-style Latin transliteration (for example, `ch` for the throat sound, `kh` for the similar guttural sound, `tz` for צ, and `sh` for שׁ). Hebrew pronunciation is authoritative; Latin spelling is a learner aid.

English came from the stable vocabulary entries and the existing corpus/phrasebook translations. `UNKNOWN` is deliberate: no reliable per-word English gloss was available; it is not a guessed translation. Current counts are recorded below.

| [adon-olam-1-l1](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l1.mp3) | אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ בְּטֶרֶם כָּל יְצִיר נִבְרָא׃ | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created. | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created.
| [adon-olam-1-l10](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l10.mp3) | וְעִם רוּחִי גְּוִיָּתִי אֲדֹנָי לִי וְלֹא אִירָא׃ | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear. | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear.
| [adon-olam-1-l2](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l2.mp3) | לְעֵת נַעֲשָׂה בְחֶפְצוֹ כֹּל אֲזַי מֶלֶךְ שְׁמוֹ נִקְרָא׃ | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed. | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed.
| [adon-olam-1-l3](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l3.mp3) | וְאַחֲרֵי כִּכְלוֹת הַכֹּל לְבַדּוֹ יִמְלֹךְ נוֹרָא׃ | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign. | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign.
| [adon-olam-1-l4](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l4.mp3) | וְהוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה בְּתִפְאָרָה׃ | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory. | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory.
| [adon-olam-1-l5](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l5.mp3) | וְהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי לְהַמְשִׁיל לוֹ לְהַחְבִּירָה׃ | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him. | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him.
| [adon-olam-1-l6](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l6.mp3) | בְּלִי רֵאשִׁית בְּלִי תַכְלִית וְלוֹ הָעֹז וְהַמִּשְׂרָה׃ | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion. | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion.
| [adon-olam-1-l7](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l7.mp3) | וְהוּא אֵלִי וְחַי גוֹאֲלִי וְצוּר חֶבְלִי בְּעֵת צָרָה׃ | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress. | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress.

| [aleinu-1](f/israeli/line/aleinu-1.mp3) | עָלֵינוּ לְשַׁבֵּחַ לַאֲדוֹן הַכֹּל לָתֵת גְּדֻלָּה לְיוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂנוּ כְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת וְלֹא שָׂמָנוּ כְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹת הָאֲדָמָה שֶׁלֹּא שָׂם חֶלְקֵנוּ כָּהֶם וְגוֹרָלֵנוּ כְּכָל־הֲמוֹנָם שֶׁהֵם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לָהֶבֶל וָרִיק וּמִתְפַּלְּלִים אֶל־אֵל לֹא יוֹשִׁיעַ וַאֲנַחְנוּ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם וְיוֹסֵד אָרֶץ וּמוֹשַׁב יְקָרוֹ בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וּשְׁכִינַת עֻזּוֹ בְּגָבְהֵי מְרוֹמִים הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְאֵין עוֹד אַחֵר אֱמֶת מַלְכֵּנוּ וְאֶפֶס זוּלָתוֹ כַּכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ אֶל־לְבָבֶךָ כִּי אֲדֹנָי הוּא הָאֱלֹהִים בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וְעַל־הָאָרֶץ מִתָּחַת אֵין עוֹד׃ | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. As it is written in the Torah: "You shall know this day and take it to heart that the LORD is God, in the heavens above and on the earth beneath; there is no other." | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. As it is written in the Torah: "You shall know this day and take it to heart that the LORD is God, in the heavens above and on the earth beneath; there is no other."

Act for the sake of your name; act for the sake of your right hand; act for the sake of your holiness; act for the sake of your Torah. That your beloved may be rescued, save with your right hand and answer me. May the words of my mouth and the thoughts of my heart be acceptable before you, LORD, my rock and my redeemer. | My God, guard my tongue from evil and my lips from speaking deceit. As for all who plan evil against me, quickly annul their counsel and frustrate their plans. Act for the sake of your name; act for the sake of your right hand; act for the sake of your holiness; act for the sake of your Torah. That your beloved may be rescued, save with your right hand and answer me. May the words of my mouth and the thoughts of my heart be acceptable before you, LORD, my rock and my redeemer.

| [brich-shmei](f/israeli/line/brich-shmei.mp3) | בְּרִיךְ שְׁמֵהּ דְּמָרֵא עָלְמָא בְּרִיךְ כִּתְרָךְ וְאַתְרָךְ יְהֵא רְעוּתָךְ עִם עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעָלַם וּפֻרְקַן יְמִינָךְ אַחֲזֵי לְעַמָּךְ בְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁךְ וּלְאַמְטוּיֵי לָנָא מִטּוּב נְהוֹרָךְ וּלְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתָנָא בְּרַחֲמִין יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתוֹרִיךְ לָן חַיִּין בְּטִיבוּ וְלֶהֱוֵי אֲנָא פְקִידָא בְּגוֹ צַדִּיקַיָּא לְמִרְחַם עָלַי וּלְמִנְטַר יָתִי וְיַת־כָּל־דִּי־לִי וְדִי־לְעַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אַנְתְּ הוּא זָן לְכֹלָּא וּמְפַרְנֵס לְכֹלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא שַׁלִּיט עַל־כֹּלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא דְּשַׁלִּיט עַל־מַלְכַיָּא וּמַלְכוּתָא דִּילָךְ הִיא אֲנָא עַבְדָּא דְקֻדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא דְּסָגִידְנָא קַמֵּהּ וּמִקַּמֵּי דִּיקַר אוֹרַיְתֵהּ בְּכָל־עִדָּן וְעִדָּן לָא עַל־אֱנָשׁ רָחִיצְנָא וְלָא עַל־בַּר־אֱלָהִין סָמִיכְנָא אֶלָּא בֵּאלָהָא דִשְׁמַיָּא דְּהוּא אֱלָהָא קְשׁוֹט וְאוֹרַיְתֵהּ קְשׁוֹט וּנְבִיאוֹהִי קְשׁוֹט וּמַסְגֵּא לְמֶעְבַּד טָבְוָן וּקְשׁוֹט בֵּהּ אֲנָא רָחִיץ וְלִשְׁמֵהּ קַדִּישָׁא יַקִּירָא אֲנָא אֵמַר תֻּשְׁבְּחָן יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתִפְתַּח לִבָּאי בְּאוֹרַיְתָא וְתַשְׁלִים מִשְׁאֲלִין דְּלִבָּאי וְלִבָּא דְכָל־עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְטָב וּלְחַיִּין וְלִשְׁלָם׃ | Blessed is the name of the Master of the world; blessed is your crown and your place. May your good will be with your people Israel for ever; show your people the redemption of your right hand in your Temple, bring us of the goodness of your light, and accept our prayer in mercy. May it be your will to lengthen our life in goodness, and let me be counted among the righteous, so that you have mercy on me and guard me and all that is mine and your people Israel's. You feed and sustain all; you rule over all; you rule over kings, and the kingdom is yours. I am a servant of the Holy One, blessed be he, before whom and before whose glorious Torah I bow at every time. Not in any person do I trust, and not on any angel do I rely, but on the God of heaven, who is the God of truth, whose Torah is truth and whose prophets are truth, and who abounds in deeds of goodness and truth. In him I trust, and to his holy and glorious name I utter praises. May it be your will to open my heart to Torah, to fulfil the requests of my heart and the heart of all your people Israel, for good, for life and for peace. | Blessed is the name of the Master of the world; blessed is your crown and your place. May your good will be with your people Israel for ever; show your people the redemption of your right hand in your Temple, bring us of the goodness of your light, and accept our prayer in mercy. May it be your will to lengthen our life in goodness, and let me be counted among the righteous, so that you have mercy on me and guard me and all that is mine and your people Israel's. You feed and sustain all; you rule over all; you rule over kings, and the kingdom is yours. I am a servant of the Holy One, blessed be he, before whom and before whose glorious Torah I bow at every time. Not in any person do I trust, and not on any angel do I rely, but on the God of heaven, who is the God of truth, whose Torah is truth and whose prophets are truth, and who abounds in deeds of goodness and truth. In him I trust, and to his holy and glorious name I utter praises. May it be your will to open my heart to Torah, to fulfil the requests of my heart and the heart of all your people Israel, for good, for life and for peace.

| [haftarah-after-l1](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם צוּר כָּל־הָעוֹלָמִים צַדִּיק בְּכָל־הַדּוֹרוֹת הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן הָאֹמֵר וְעֹשֶׂה הַמְדַבֵּר וּמְקַיֵּם שֶׁכָּל־דְּבָרָיו אֱמֶת וָצֶדֶק נֶאֱמָן אַתָּה הוּא אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְנֶאֱמָנִים דְּבָרֶיךָ וְדָבָר אֶחָד מִדְּבָרֶיךָ אָחוֹר לֹא יָשׁוּב רֵיקָם כִּי אֵל מֶלֶךְ נֶאֱמָן וְרַחֲמָן אָתָּה בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן בְּכָל־דְּבָרָיו׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, rock of all the worlds, righteous in all generations, the faithful God who says and does, who speaks and fulfils, all of whose words are true and just. You are faithful, LORD our God, and your words are faithful, and not one of your words returns empty, for you are a faithful and merciful God and king. Blessed are you, LORD, the God who is faithful in all his words. | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, rock of all the worlds, righteous in all generations, the faithful God who says and does, who speaks and fulfils, all of whose words are true and just. You are faithful, LORD our God, and your words are faithful, and not one of your words returns empty, for you are a faithful and merciful God and king. Blessed are you, LORD, the God who is faithful in all his words.

| [haftarah-after-l3](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l3.mp3) | שַׂמְּחֵנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּאֵלִיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא עַבְדָּךְ וּבְמַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד מְשִׁיחָךְ בִּמְהֵרָה יָבֹא וְיָגֵל לִבֵּנוּ עַל כִּסְאוֹ לֹא יֵשֵׁב זָר וְלֹא יִנְחֲלוּ עוֹד אֲחֵרִים אֶת כְּבוֹדוֹ כִּי בְשֵׁם קָדְשְׁךָ נִשְׁבַּעְתָּ לּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִכְבֶּה נֵרוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מָגֵן דָּוִד׃ | Gladden us, LORD our God, with Elijah the prophet your servant and with the kingdom of the house of David your anointed; may he come quickly and make our heart rejoice. | [kaddish-1-l6](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l6.mp3) | יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן־שְׁמַיָּא וְחַיִּים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל־כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | May there be great peace from heaven, and life, for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen. | May there be great peace from heaven, and life, for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen.

| [kedushah-kadosh](f/israeli/line/kedushah-kadosh.mp3) | וְקָרָא זֶה אֶל־זֶה וְאָמַר קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ אֲדֹנָי צְבָאוֹת מְלֹא כָל־הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדוֹ׃ | And one called unto another, and said: Holy, holy, holy, is the LORD of hosts; The whole earth is full of His glory. | And one called unto another, and said: Holy, holy, holy, is the LORD of hosts; The whole earth is full of His glory.
| [kedushah-yimloch](f/israeli/line/kedushah-yimloch.mp3) | יִמְלֹךְ אֲדֹנָי לְעוֹלָם אֱלֹהַיִךְ צִיּוֹן לְדֹר וָדֹר הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃ | The LORD will reign for ever, Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Hallelujah. | The LORD will reign for ever, Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Hallelujah.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1.mp3) | וַיְכֻלּוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ וְכָל־צְבָאָם׃ | And the heaven and the earth were finished, and all the host of them. | And the heaven and the earth were finished, and all the host of them.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2.mp3) | וַיְכַל אֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה וַיִּשְׁבֹּת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה׃ | And on the seventh day God finished His work which He had made; and He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made. | And on the seventh day God finished His work which He had made; and He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3.mp3) | וַיְבָרֶךְ אֱלֹהִים אֶת־יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי וַיְקַדֵּשׁ אֹתוֹ כִּי בוֹ שָׁבַת מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר־בָּרָא אֱלֹהִים לַעֲשׂוֹת׃ | And God blessed the seventh day, and hallowed it; because that in it He rested from all His work which God in creating had made. | And God blessed the seventh day, and hallowed it; because that in it He rested from all His work which God in creating had made.
| [kiddush-shabbat-eve](f/israeli/line/kiddush-shabbat-eve.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְרָצָה בָנוּ וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשׁוֹ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחִילָנוּ זִכָּרוֹן לְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית כִּי הוּא יוֹם תְּחִלָּה לְמִקְרָאֵי קֹדֶשׁ זֵכֶר לִיצִיאַת מִצְרָיִם כִּי בָנוּ בָחַרְתָּ וְאוֹתָנוּ קִדַּשְׁתָּ מִכָּל הָעַמִּים וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשְׁךָ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחַלְתָּנוּ׃ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who made us holy with his commandments and was pleased with us, and in love and favour gave us his holy Shabbat as an inheritance, a remembrance of the work of creation. | [kiddush-veshamru-l1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l1.mp3) | וְשָׁמְרוּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לַעֲשׂוֹת אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לְדֹרֹתָם בְּרִית עוֹלָם׃ | Wherefore the children of Israel shall keep the sabbath, to observe the sabbath throughout their generations, for a perpetual covenant. | Wherefore the children of Israel shall keep the sabbath, to observe the sabbath throughout their generations, for a perpetual covenant.
| [kiddush-veshamru-l2](f/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l2.mp3) | בֵּינִי וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹת הִוא לְעֹלָם כִּי־שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים עָשָׂה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת־הָאָרֶץ וּבַיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שָׁבַת וַיִּנָּפַשׁ׃ | It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel for ever; for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He ceased from work and rested.’ | It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel for ever; for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He ceased from work and rested.’
| [kiddush-wine-1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-wine-1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הַגָּפֶן׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates the fruit of the vine. | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates the fruit of the vine.

| [shema-vahavta-l2](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l2.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might.
| [shema-vahavta-l3](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l3.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart;
| [shema-vahavta-l4](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l4.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up.
| [shema-vahavta-l5](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l5.mp3) | וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל־יָדֶךָ וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy hand, and they shall be for frontlets between thine eyes. | And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy hand, and they shall be for frontlets between thine eyes.
| [shema-vahavta-l6](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l6.mp3) | וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates. | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates.
| [shir-hamaalot-l1](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l1.mp3) | שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת בְּשׁוּב אֲדֹנָי אֶת־שִׁיבַת צִיּוֹן הָיִינוּ כְּחֹלְמִים׃ | A Song of Ascents. When the LORD brought back those that returned to Zion, We were like unto them that dream. | A Song of Ascents. When the LORD brought back those that returned to Zion, We were like unto them that dream.
| [shir-hamaalot-l2](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l2.mp3) | אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם־אֵלֶּה׃ | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.' | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.'
| [shir-hamaalot-l3](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l3.mp3) | הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִמָּנוּ הָיִינוּ שְׂמֵחִים׃ | The LORD hath done great things with us; we are rejoiced. | The LORD hath done great things with us; we are rejoiced.
| [shir-hamaalot-l4](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l4.mp3) | שׁוּבָה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־שְׁבִיתֵנוּ כַּאֲפִיקִים בַּנֶּגֶב׃ | Turn our captivity, O LORD, as the streams in the dry land. | Turn our captivity, O LORD, as the streams in the dry land.
| [shir-hamaalot-l5](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l5.mp3) | הַזֹּרְעִים בְּדִמְעָה בְּרִנָּה יִקְצֹרוּ׃ | They that sow in tears Shall reap in joy. | They that sow in tears Shall reap in joy.
| [shir-hamaalot-l6](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l6.mp3) | הָלוֹךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָכֹה נֹשֵׂא מֶשֶׁךְ־הַזָּרַע בֹּא־יָבוֹא בְרִנָּה נֹשֵׂא אֲלֻמֹּתָיו׃ | Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves. | Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves.

| [sim-shalom-l1](f/israeli/line/sim-shalom-l1.mp3) | שִׂים שָׁלוֹם טוֹבָה וּבְרָכָה חֵן וָחֶסֶד וְרַחֲמִים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּךָ בָּרְכֵנוּ אָבִינוּ כֻּלָּנוּ כְּאֶחָד בְּאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ כִּי בְאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ נָתַתָּ לָּנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ תּוֹרַת חַיִּים וְאַהֲבַת חֶסֶד וּצְדָקָה וּבְרָכָה וְרַחֲמִים וְחַיִּים וְשָׁלוֹם וְטוֹב בְּעֵינֶיךָ לְבָרֵךְ אֶת עַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־עֵת וּבְכָל־שָׁעָה בִּשְׁלוֹמֶךָ׃ | Grant peace, goodness and blessing, grace, kindness and mercy upon us and upon all Israel your people. Bless us, our father, all of us as one, with the light of your face; for by the light of your face you gave us, LORD our God, a Torah of life, love of kindness, righteousness, blessing, mercy, life and peace. | [vahavta-full-l5](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l5.mp3) | וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates. | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates.
| [vayehi-binsoa](f/israeli/line/vayehi-binsoa.mp3) | וַיְהִי בִּנְסֹעַ הָאָרֹן וַיֹאמֶר מֹשֶׁה קוּמָה אֲדֹנָי וְיָפֻצוּ אֹיְבֶיךָ וְיָנֻסוּ מְשַׂנְאֶיךָ מִפָּנֶיךָ׃ כִּי מִצִּיּוֹן תֵּצֵא תוֹרָה וּדְבַר אֲדֹנָי מִירוּשָׁלָםִ׃ בָּרוּךְ שֶׁנָּתַן תּוֹרָה לְעַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּקְדֻשָּׁתוֹ׃ | And it was, when the ark set out, Moses said: Arise, LORD, and let your enemies be scattered, and let those who hate you flee before you. For from Zion will go forth Torah, and the word of the LORD from Jerusalem. Blessed is the one who gave the Torah to his people Israel in his holiness. | And it was, when the ark set out, Moses said: Arise, LORD, and let your enemies be scattered, and let those who hate you flee before you. For from Zion will go forth Torah, and the word of the LORD from Jerusalem. Blessed is the one who gave the Torah to his people Israel in his holiness.
| [vezot-hatorah](f/israeli/line/vezot-hatorah.mp3) | וְזֹאת הַתּוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר שָׂם מֹשֶׁה לִפְנֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל׃ עַל פִּי אֲדֹנָי בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה׃ | This is the Torah that Moses set before the children of Israel, by the word of the LORD, by the hand of Moses. | This is the Torah that Moses set before the children of Israel, by the word of the LORD, by the hand of Moses.
| [yehalelu-l1](f/israeli/line/yehalelu-l1.mp3) | יְהַלְלוּ אֶת־שֵׁם אֲדֹנָי כִּי־נִשְׂגָּב שְׁמוֹ לְבַדּוֹ הוֹדוֹ עַל־אֶרֶץ וְשָׁמָיִם׃ | Let them praise the name of the LORD, For His name alone is exalted; His glory is above the earth and heaven. | Let them praise the name of the LORD, For His name alone is exalted; His glory is above the earth and heaven.
| [yehalelu-l2](f/israeli/line/yehalelu-l2.mp3) | וַיָּרֶם קֶרֶן לְעַמּוֹ תְּהִלָּה לְכָל־חֲסִידָיו לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַם־קְרֹבוֹ הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃ | And He hath lifted up a horn for His people, A praise for all His saints, Even for the children of Israel, a people near unto Him. Hallelujah. | And He hath lifted up a horn for His people, A praise for all His saints, Even for the children of Israel, a people near unto Him. Hallelujah.
| [yigdal-l1](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l1.mp3) | יִגְדַּל אֱלֹהִים חַי וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח נִמְצָא וְאֵין עֵת אֶל מְצִיאוּתוֹ׃ | May the living God be magnified and praised; he exists, and his existence is not bound by time. | May the living God be magnified and praised; he exists, and his existence is not bound by time.
| [yigdal-l10](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l10.mp3) | צוֹפֶה וְיוֹדֵעַ סְתָרֵינוּ מַבִּיט לְסוֹף דָּבָר בְּקַדְמָתוֹ׃ | He watches and knows our secrets; he sees the end of a matter at its beginning. | He watches and knows our secrets; he sees the end of a matter at its beginning.
| [yigdal-l11](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l11.mp3) | גּוֹמֵל לְאִישׁ חֶסֶד כְּמִפְעָלוֹ נוֹתֵן לְרָשָׁע רָע כְּרִשְׁעָתוֹ׃ | He repays a person with kindness according to his deeds; he gives the wicked evil according to his wickedness. | He repays a person with kindness according to his deeds; he gives the wicked evil according to his wickedness.
| [yigdal-l12](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l12.mp3) | יִשְׁלַח לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין מְשִׁיחֵנוּ לִפְדּוֹת מְחַכֵּי קֵץ יְשׁוּעָתוֹ׃ | At the end of days he will send our anointed one, to redeem those who await the end of his salvation. | At the end of days he will send our anointed one, to redeem those who await the end of his salvation.
| [yigdal-l13](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l13.mp3) | מֵתִים יְחַיֶּה אֵל בְּרֹב חַסְדּוֹ בָּרוּךְ עֲדֵי עַד שֵׁם תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃ | God will revive the dead in his great kindness; blessed for ever is his praised name. | God will revive the dead in his great kindness; blessed for ever is his praised name.
| [yigdal-l2](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l2.mp3) | אֶחָד וְאֵין יָחִיד כְּיִחוּדוֹ נֶעְלָם וְגַם אֵין סוֹף לְאַחְדּוּתוֹ׃ | He is one, and there is no unity like his unity; hidden, and without end to his oneness. | He is one, and there is no unity like his unity; hidden, and without end to his oneness.
| [yigdal-l3](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l3.mp3) | אֵין לוֹ דְמוּת הַגּוּף וְאֵינוֹ גוּף לֹא נַעֲרֹךְ אֵלָיו קְדֻשָּׁתוֹ׃ | He has no bodily form and is not a body; his holiness cannot be compared. | He has no bodily form and is not a body; his holiness cannot be compared.
| [yigdal-l4](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l4.mp3) | קַדְמוֹן לְכָל־דָּבָר אֲשֶׁר נִבְרָא רִאשׁוֹן וְאֵין רֵאשִׁית לְרֵאשִׁיתוֹ׃ | He preceded everything that was created; first, and with no beginning to his beginning. | He preceded everything that was created; first, and with no beginning to his beginning.
| [yigdal-l5](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l5.mp3) | הִנּוֹ אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם לְכָל־נוֹצָר יוֹרֶה גְדֻלָּתוֹ וּמַלְכוּתוֹ׃ | Behold, he is master of the world to every creature; he shows his greatness and his sovereignty. | Behold, he is master of the world to every creature; he shows his greatness and his sovereignty.
| [yigdal-l6](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l6.mp3) | שֶׁפַע נְבוּאָתוֹ נְתָנוֹ אֶל אַנְשֵׁי סְגֻלָּתוֹ וְתִפְאַרְתּוֹ׃ | The abundance of his prophecy he gave to the people he treasured and who are his glory. | The abundance of his prophecy he gave to the people he treasured and who are his glory.
| [yigdal-l7](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l7.mp3) | לֹא קָם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמֹשֶׁה עוֹד נָבִיא וּמַבִּיט אֶת־תְּמוּנָתוֹ׃ | No one has arisen in Israel like Moses, a prophet who beheld his likeness. | No one has arisen in Israel like Moses, a prophet who beheld his likeness.
| [yigdal-l8](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l8.mp3) | תּוֹרַת אֱמֶת נָתַן לְעַמּוֹ אֵל עַל יַד נְבִיאוֹ נֶאֱמַן בֵּיתוֹ׃ | A Torah of truth God gave to his people through his prophet, trusted in his house. | A Torah of truth God gave to his people through his prophet, trusted in his house.
| [yigdal-l9](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l9.mp3) | לֹא יַחֲלִיף הָאֵל וְלֹא יָמִיר דָּתוֹ לְעוֹלָמִים לְזוּלָתוֹ׃ | God will not alter or exchange his law, for ever, for any other. | God will not alter or exchange his law, for ever, for any other.
| [zimun-opening-l1](f/israeli/line/zimun-opening-l1.mp3) | רַבּוֹתַי נְבָרֵךְ׃ | Gentlemen, let us bless. | Gentlemen, let us bless.
| [zimun-opening-l2](f/israeli/line/zimun-opening-l2.mp3) | יְהִי שֵׁם אֲדֹנָי מְבֹרָךְ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד־עוֹלָם׃ | May the name of the LORD be blessed from now and for ever. | May the name of the LORD be blessed from now and for ever.
| [adon-olam-1-l1](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l1.mp3) | אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ בְּטֶרֶם כָּל יְצִיר נִבְרָא׃ | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created. | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created.
| [adon-olam-1-l10](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l10.mp3) | וְעִם רוּחִי גְּוִיָּתִי אֲדֹנָי לִי וְלֹא אִירָא׃ | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear. | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear.
| [adon-olam-1-l2](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l2.mp3) | לְעֵת נַעֲשָׂה בְחֶפְצוֹ כֹּל אֲזַי מֶלֶךְ שְׁמוֹ נִקְרָא׃ | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed. | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed.
| [adon-olam-1-l3](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l3.mp3) | וְאַחֲרֵי כִּכְלוֹת הַכֹּל לְבַדּוֹ יִמְלֹךְ נוֹרָא׃ | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign. | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign.
| [adon-olam-1-l4](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l4.mp3) | וְהוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה בְּתִפְאָרָה׃ | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory. | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory.
| [adon-olam-1-l5](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l5.mp3) | וְהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי לְהַמְשִׁיל לוֹ לְהַחְבִּירָה׃ | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him. | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him.
| [adon-olam-1-l6](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l6.mp3) | בְּלִי רֵאשִׁית בְּלִי תַכְלִית וְלוֹ הָעֹז וְהַמִּשְׂרָה׃ | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion. | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion.
| [adon-olam-1-l7](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l7.mp3) | וְהוּא אֵלִי וְחַי גוֹאֲלִי וְצוּר חֶבְלִי בְּעֵת צָרָה׃ | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress. | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress.
| [adon-olam-1-l8](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l8.mp3) | וְהוּא נִסִּי וּמָנוֹס לִי מְנָת כּוֹסִי בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא׃ | And he is my banner and my refuge, the portion of my cup on the day I call. | And he is my banner and my refuge, the portion of my cup on the day I call.
| [adon-olam-1-l9](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l9.mp3) | בְּיָדוֹ אַפְקִיד רוּחִי בְּעֵת אִישָׁן וְאָעִירָה׃ | Into his hand I commit my spirit, when I sleep and when I wake. | Into his hand I commit my spirit, when I sleep and when I wake.
| [adonai-oz](m/israeli/line/adonai-oz.mp3) | אֲדֹנָי עֹז לְעַמּוֹ יִתֵּן אֲדֹנָי יְבָרֵךְ אֶת־עַמּוֹ בַשָּׁלוֹם׃ | The LORD will give strength unto His people; The LORD will bless his people with peace. | The LORD will give strength unto His people; The LORD will bless his people with peace.
| [aleinu-1](m/israeli/line/aleinu-1.mp3) | עָלֵינוּ לְשַׁבֵּחַ לַאֲדוֹן הַכֹּל לָתֵת גְּדֻלָּה לְיוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂנוּ כְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת וְלֹא שָׂמָנוּ כְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹת הָאֲדָמָה שֶׁלֹּא שָׂם חֶלְקֵנוּ כָּהֶם וְגוֹרָלֵנוּ כְּכָל־הֲמוֹנָם שֶׁהֵם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לָהֶבֶל וָרִיק וּמִתְפַּלְּלִים אֶל־אֵל לֹא יוֹשִׁיעַ וַאֲנַחְנוּ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם וְיוֹסֵד אָרֶץ וּמוֹשַׁב יְקָרוֹ בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וּשְׁכִינַת עֻזּוֹ בְּגָבְהֵי מְרוֹמִים הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְאֵין עוֹד אַחֵר אֱמֶת מַלְכֵּנוּ וְאֶפֶס זוּלָתוֹ כַּכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ אֶל־לְבָבֶךָ כִּי אֲדֹנָי הוּא הָאֱלֹהִים בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וְעַל־הָאָרֶץ מִתָּחַת אֵין עוֹד׃ | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. As it is written in the Torah: "You shall know this day and take it to heart that the LORD is God, in the heavens above and on the earth beneath; there is no other." | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. As it is written in the Torah: "You shall know this day and take it to heart that the LORD is God, in the heavens above and on the earth beneath; there is no other."
| [amidah-elohai-netzor](m/israeli/line/amidah-elohai-netzor.mp3) | אֱלֹהַי נְצוֹר לְשׁוֹנִי מֵרָע וּשְׂפָתַי מִדַּבֵּר מִרְמָה וְלִמְקַלְלַי נַפְשִׁי תִדּוֹם וְנַפְשִׁי כֶּעָפָר לַכֹּל תִּהְיֶה פְּתַח לִבִּי בְּתוֹרָתֶךָ וּבְמִצְוֹתֶיךָ תִּרְדֹּף נַפְשִׁי וְכֹל הַחוֹשְׁבִים עָלַי רָעָה מְהֵרָה הָפֵר עֲצָתָם וְקַלְקֵל מַחֲשַׁבְתָּם׃ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן יְמִינֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן קְדֻשָּׁתֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן תּוֹרָתֶךָ לְמַעַן יֵחָלְצוּן יְדִידֶיךָ הוֹשִׁיעָה יְמִינְךָ וַעֲנֵנִי׃ יִהְיוּ לְרָצוֹן אִמְרֵי פִי וְהֶגְיוֹן לִבִּי לְפָנֶיךָ אֲדֹנָי צוּרִי וְגוֹאֲלִי׃ | My God, guard my tongue from evil and my lips from speaking deceit. To those who curse me, let my soul be silent; let my soul be like dust to all. Open my heart to your Torah, and let my soul pursue your commandments. As for all who plan evil against me, quickly annul their counsel and frustrate their plans. Act for the sake of your name; act for the sake of your right hand; act for the sake of your holiness; act for the sake of your Torah. That your beloved may be rescued, save with your right hand and answer me. May the words of my mouth and the thoughts of my heart be acceptable before you, LORD, my rock and my redeemer. | My God, guard my tongue from evil and my lips from speaking deceit. To those who curse me, let my soul be silent; let my soul be like dust to all. Open my heart to your Torah, and let my soul pursue your commandments. Act for the sake of your name; act for the sake of your right hand; act for the sake of your holiness; act for the sake of your Torah. | [brich-shmei](m/israeli/line/brich-shmei.mp3) | בְּרִיךְ שְׁמֵהּ דְּמָרֵא עָלְמָא בְּרִיךְ כִּתְרָךְ וְאַתְרָךְ יְהֵא רְעוּתָךְ עִם עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעָלַם וּפֻרְקַן יְמִינָךְ אַחֲזֵי לְעַמָּךְ בְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁךְ וּלְאַמְטוּיֵי לָנָא מִטּוּב נְהוֹרָךְ וּלְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתָנָא בְּרַחֲמִין יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתוֹרִיךְ לָן חַיִּין בְּטִיבוּ וְלֶהֱוֵי אֲנָא פְקִידָא בְּגוֹ צַדִּיקַיָּא לְמִרְחַם עָלַי וּלְמִנְטַר יָתִי וְיַת־כָּל־דִּי־לִי וְדִי־לְעַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אַנְתְּ הוּא זָן לְכֹלָּא וּמְפַרְנֵס לְכֹלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא שַׁלִּיט עַל־כֹּלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא דְּשַׁלִּיט עַל־מַלְכַיָּא וּמַלְכוּתָא דִּילָךְ הִיא אֲנָא עַבְדָּא דְקֻדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא דְּסָגִידְנָא קַמֵּהּ וּמִקַּמֵּי דִּיקַר אוֹרַיְתֵהּ בְּכָל־עִדָּן וְעִדָּן לָא עַל־אֱנָשׁ רָחִיצְנָא וְלָא עַל־בַּר־אֱלָהִין סָמִיכְנָא אֶלָּא בֵּאלָהָא דִשְׁמַיָּא דְּהוּא אֱלָהָא קְשׁוֹט וְאוֹרַיְתֵהּ קְשׁוֹט וּנְבִיאוֹהִי קְשׁוֹט וּמַסְגֵּא לְמֶעְבַּד טָבְוָן וּקְשׁוֹט בֵּהּ אֲנָא רָחִיץ וְלִשְׁמֵהּ קַדִּישָׁא יַקִּירָא אֲנָא אֵמַר תֻּשְׁבְּחָן יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתִפְתַּח לִבָּאי בְּאוֹרַיְתָא וְתַשְׁלִים מִשְׁאֲלִין דְּלִבָּאי וְלִבָּא דְכָל־עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְטָב וּלְחַיִּין וְלִשְׁלָם׃ | Blessed is the name of the Master of the world; blessed is your crown and your place. | [hanerot-halalu](m/israeli/line/hanerot-halalu.mp3) | הַנֵּרוֹת הַלָּלוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מַדְלִיקִין עַל הַנִּסִּים וְעַל הַתְּשׁוּעוֹת וְעַל הַנִּפְלָאוֹת שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ לַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ עַל יְדֵי כֹּהֲנֶיךָ הַקְּדוֹשִׁים וְכָל־שְׁמוֹנַת יְמֵי חֲנֻכָּה הַנֵּרוֹת הַלָּלוּ קֹדֶשׁ וְאֵין לָנוּ רְשׁוּת לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶם אֶלָּא לִרְאוֹתָם בִּלְבָד כְּדֵי לְהוֹדוֹת לִשְׁמֶךָ עַל נִסֶּיךָ נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ וִישׁוּעוֹתֶיךָ׃ | We light these lights for the miracles, the deliverances, and the wonders that you did for our ancestors through your holy priests. | [havdalah-spices](m/israeli/line/havdalah-spices.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם בּוֹרֵא מִינֵי בְשָׂמִים׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates kinds of spices. | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates kinds of spices.

| [kaddish-1-l2](m/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l2.mp3) | בְּעָלְמָא דִּי־בְרָא כִרְעוּתֵהּ וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | In the world that he created by his will; may he establish his kingdom in your lifetime and in your days and in the lifetime of all the house of Israel, speedily and soon; and say: Amen. | In the world that he created by his will; may he establish his kingdom in your lifetime and in your days and in the lifetime of all the house of Israel, speedily and soon; and say: Amen.

| [kaddish-1-l4](m/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l4.mp3) | יִתְבָּרַךְ וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח וְיִתְפָּאַר וְיִתְרוֹמַם וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא וְיִתְהַדָּר וְיִתְעַלֶּה וְיִתְהַלָּל שְׁמֵהּ דְקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא | Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honoured, raised and lauded be the name of the Holy One, blessed be he, | Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honoured, raised and lauded be the name of the Holy One, blessed be he,
| [kaddish-1-l5](m/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l5.mp3) | לְעֵלָּא מִן־כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא וְשִׁירָתָא תֻּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָלְמָא וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | beyond all the blessings and songs, praises and consolations that are uttered in the world; and say: Amen. | beyond all the blessings and songs, praises and consolations that are uttered in the world; and say: Amen.
| [kaddish-1-l6](m/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l6.mp3) | יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן־שְׁמַיָּא וְחַיִּים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל־כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | May there be great peace from heaven, and life, for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen. | May there be great peace from heaven, and life, for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen.
| [kaddish-1-l7](m/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l7.mp3) | עוֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו הוּא יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | He who makes peace in his high places, may he make peace for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen. | He who makes peace in his high places, may he make peace for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen.
| [kedushah-baruch](m/israeli/line/kedushah-baruch.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ כְּבוֹד־אֲדֹנָי מִמְּקוֹמוֹ׃ | Blessed be the glory of the LORD from his place. | Blessed be the glory of the LORD from his place.
| [kedushah-kadosh](m/israeli/line/kedushah-kadosh.mp3) | וְקָרָא זֶה אֶל־זֶה וְאָמַר קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ אֲדֹנָי צְבָאוֹת מְלֹא כָל־הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדוֹ׃ | And one called unto another, and said: Holy, holy, holy, is the LORD of hosts; The whole earth is full of His glory. | And one called unto another, and said: Holy, holy, holy, is the LORD of hosts; The whole earth is full of His glory.
| [kedushah-yimloch](m/israeli/line/kedushah-yimloch.mp3) | יִמְלֹךְ אֲדֹנָי לְעוֹלָם אֱלֹהַיִךְ צִיּוֹן לְדֹר וָדֹר הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃ | The LORD will reign for ever, Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Hallelujah. | The LORD will reign for ever, Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Hallelujah.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1](m/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1.mp3) | וַיְכֻלּוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ וְכָל־צְבָאָם׃ | And the heaven and the earth were finished, and all the host of them. | And the heaven and the earth were finished, and all the host of them.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2](m/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2.mp3) | וַיְכַל אֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה וַיִּשְׁבֹּת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה׃ | And on the seventh day God finished His work which He had made; and He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made. | And on the seventh day God finished His work which He had made; and He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made.
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3](m/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3.mp3) | וַיְבָרֶךְ אֱלֹהִים אֶת־יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי וַיְקַדֵּשׁ אֹתוֹ כִּי בוֹ שָׁבַת מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר־בָּרָא אֱלֹהִים לַעֲשׂוֹת׃ | And God blessed the seventh day, and hallowed it; because that in it He rested from all His work which God in creating had made. | And God blessed the seventh day, and hallowed it; because that in it He rested from all His work which God in creating had made.
| [kiddush-shabbat-eve](m/israeli/line/kiddush-shabbat-eve.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְרָצָה בָנוּ וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשׁוֹ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחִילָנוּ זִכָּרוֹן לְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית כִּי הוּא יוֹם תְּחִלָּה לְמִקְרָאֵי קֹדֶשׁ זֵכֶר לִיצִיאַת מִצְרָיִם כִּי בָנוּ בָחַרְתָּ וְאוֹתָנוּ קִדַּשְׁתָּ מִכָּל הָעַמִּים וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשְׁךָ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחַלְתָּנוּ׃ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who made us holy with his commandments and was pleased with us, and in love and favour gave us his holy Shabbat as an inheritance, a remembrance of the work of creation. | [kiddush-veshamru-l1](m/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l1.mp3) | וְשָׁמְרוּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לַעֲשׂוֹת אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לְדֹרֹתָם בְּרִית עוֹלָם׃ | Wherefore the children of Israel shall keep the sabbath, to observe the sabbath throughout their generations, for a perpetual covenant. | Wherefore the children of Israel shall keep the sabbath, to observe the sabbath throughout their generations, for a perpetual covenant.
| [kiddush-veshamru-l2](m/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l2.mp3) | בֵּינִי וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹת הִוא לְעֹלָם כִּי־שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים עָשָׂה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת־הָאָרֶץ וּבַיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שָׁבַת וַיִּנָּפַשׁ׃ | It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel for ever; for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He ceased from work and rested.’ | It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel for ever; for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He ceased from work and rested.’

| [kiddush-zachor-l2](m/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l2.mp3) | שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים תַּעֲבֹד וְעָשִׂיתָ כָּל־מְלַאכְתֶּךָ | Six days shalt thou labour, and do all thy work; | Six days shalt thou labour, and do all thy work;
| [kiddush-zachor-l3](m/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l3.mp3) | וְיוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שַׁבָּת לַאֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ לֹא־תַעֲשֶׂה כָל־מְלָאכָה אַתָּה וּבִנְךָ־וּבִתֶּךָ עַבְדְּךָ וַאֲמָתְךָ וּבְהֶמְתֶּךָ וְגֵרְךָ אֲשֶׁר בִּשְׁעָרֶיךָ | but the seventh day is a sabbath unto the LORD thy God, in it thou shalt not do any manner of work, thou, nor thy son, nor thy daughter, nor thy man-servant, nor thy maid-servant, nor thy cattle, nor thy stranger that is within thy gates; | but the seventh day is a sabbath unto the LORD thy God, in it thou shalt not do any manner of work, thou, nor thy son, nor thy daughter, nor thy man-servant, nor thy maid-servant, nor thy cattle, nor thy stranger that is within thy gates;

| [shema-vahavta-l2](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l2.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might.

| [shema-vahavta-l4](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l4.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up.

| [shir-hamaalot-l2](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l2.mp3) | אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם־אֵלֶּה׃ | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.' | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.'

| [shir-hamaalot-l6](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l6.mp3) | הָלוֹךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָכֹה נֹשֵׂא מֶשֶׁךְ־הַזָּרַע בֹּא־יָבוֹא בְרִנָּה נֹשֵׂא אֲלֻמֹּתָיו׃ | Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves. | Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves.

| [vahavta-2-l1](m/israeli/line/vahavta-2-l1.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart;

| [yigdal-l2](m/israeli/line/yigdal-l2.mp3) | אֶחָד וְאֵין יָחִיד כְּיִחוּדוֹ נֶעְלָם וְגַם אֵין סוֹף לְאַחְדּוּתוֹ׃ | He is one, and there is no unity like his unity; hidden, and without end to his oneness. | He is one, and there is no unity like his unity; hidden, and without end to his oneness.

| [yigdal-l4](m/israeli/line/yigdal-l4.mp3) | קַדְמוֹן לְכָל־דָּבָר אֲשֶׁר נִבְרָא רִאשׁוֹן וְאֵין רֵאשִׁית לְרֵאשִׁיתוֹ׃ | He preceded everything that was created; first, and with no beginning to his beginning. | He preceded everything that was created; first, and with no beginning to his beginning.
| [yigdal-l5](m/israeli/line/yigdal-l5.mp3) | הִנּוֹ אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם לְכָל־נוֹצָר יוֹרֶה גְדֻלָּתוֹ וּמַלְכוּתוֹ׃ | Behold, he is master of the world to every creature; he shows his greatness and his sovereignty. | Behold, he is master of the world to every creature; he shows his greatness and his sovereignty.
| [yigdal-l6](m/israeli/line/yigdal-l6.mp3) | שֶׁפַע נְבוּאָתוֹ נְתָנוֹ אֶל אַנְשֵׁי סְגֻלָּתוֹ וְתִפְאַרְתּוֹ׃ | The abundance of his prophecy he gave to the people he treasured and who are his glory. | The abundance of his prophecy he gave to the people he treasured and who are his glory.
| [yigdal-l7](m/israeli/line/yigdal-l7.mp3) | לֹא קָם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמֹשֶׁה עוֹד נָבִיא וּמַבִּיט אֶת־תְּמוּנָתוֹ׃ | No one has arisen in Israel like Moses, a prophet who beheld his likeness. | No one has arisen in Israel like Moses, a prophet who beheld his likeness.
